Pause fleet: write migration_lock row\n67\t/// 2. Backup: copy beads.db to beads.db.br-backup-<timestamp>\n68\t/// 3. }\n133\t}\n134\t\n135\t/// Inner migration implementation (lock already held).\n136\tfn inner_migrate(\n137\t    storage: &Storage,\n138\t    beads_dir: &std::path::Path,\n139\t    db_path:            .to_string(),\n188\t                    );\n189\t                }\n190\t                if !doctor_result.issues.is_empty() {\n191\t                    for issue in &doctor_result.issues {\n197\t                verification\n198\t                    .errors\n199\t                    .push(format!(\"Backward compatibility check failed: {}\", e));\n200\t            }\n201\t        }\n202\t    }\n203\t\n204\t    println!(\"  Migration complete\");\n205\t    if config_updated {\n206\t        println!(\"  Note: config.yaml was updated with bf defaults\");\n207\t    }\n208\t\n209\t    Ok(MigrateResult {\n210\t        verification,\n211\t        imported: 0,\n212\t        events_reconstructed: cache_result,\n213\t    })\n214\t}\n215\t\n216\t/// Acquire migration lock to prevent concurrent claims during migration.\n217\tfn acquire_migration_lock(storage: &Storage, locked_by: &str) -> Result<i64> {\n218\t    let now = Utc::now();\n219\t    let expires_at = now + chrono::Duration::hours(1); // Lock expires after 1 hour\n220\t\n221\t    storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n222\t        tx.execute(\n223\t            \"INSERT OR REPLACE INTO migration_lock (id, locked_by, locked_at, expires_at)\n224\t             VALUES (1, ?1, ?2, ?3)\",\n225\t            rusqlite::params![locked_by, now.to_rfc3339(), expires_at.to_rfc3339()],\n226\t        )?;\n227\t        Ok::<_, anyhow::Error>(1)\n228\t    })\n229\t}\n230\t\n231\t/// Release migration lock after migration completes.\n232\tfn release_migration_lock(storage: &Storage, _lock_id: i64) -> Result<()> {\n233\t    storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n234\t        tx.execute(\"DELETE FROM migration_lock WHERE id = 1\", [])?;\n235\t        Ok::<_, anyhow::Error>(())\n236\t    })\n237\t}\n238\t\n239\t/// Prime critical_path_cache for all beads.\n240\tfn prime_critical_path_cache(storage: &Storage) -> Result<usize> {\n241\t    storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n242\t        // Compute all critical paths - this populates the cache\n243\t        crate::critical_path::compute_all_critical_paths(tx)?;\n244\t\n245\t        // Count how many beads were cached\n246\t        let count: i64 = tx.query_row(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM critical_path_cache\", [], |row| {\n247\t            row.get(0)\n248\t        })?;\n249\t        Ok(count as usize)\n250\t    })\n251\t}\n252\t\n253\t/// Seed config.yaml with bf-specific defaults if missing.\n254\tfn seed_config(config_path: &std::path::Path) -> Result<bool> {\n255\t    use std::io::Write;\n256\t\n257\t    // Read existing config\n258\t    let existing_content = if config_path.exists() {\n259\t        std::fs::read_to_string(config_path)?\n260\t    } else {\n261\t        String::new()\n262\t    };\n263\t\n264\t    // Check if bf defaults are already present\n265\t    let has_claim_ttl = existing_content.contains(\"claim_ttl_minutes:\");\n266\t    let has_rotate_age = existing_content.contains(\"rotate_age_days:\");\n267\t    let has_rotate_max_size = existing_content.contains(\"rotate_max_size_mb:\");\n268\t\n269\t    if has_claim_ttl && has_rotate_age && has_rotate_max_size {\n270\t        return Ok(false); // No update needed\n271\t    }\n272\t\n273\t    // Parse existing config or create new one\n274\t    let mut config: crate::config::Config = if existing_content.trim().is_empty() {\n275\t        crate::config::Config::default()\n276\t    } else {\n277\t        serde_yaml::from_str(&existing_content)?\n278\t    };\n279\t\n280\t    // Ensure bf defaults are set\n281\t    if config.claim_ttl_minutes == 0 {\n282\t        config.claim_ttl_minutes = 30;\n283\t    }\n284\t    if config.rotate.rotate_age_days == 0 {\n285\t        config.rotate.rotate_age_days = 30;\n286\t    }\n287\t    if config.rotate.rotate_max_size_mb == 0 {\n288\t        config.rotate.rotate_max_size_mb = 100;\n289\t    }\n290\t\n291\t    // Write back\n292\t    let yaml = serde_yaml::to_string(&config)?;\n293\t    let mut file = std::fs::File::create(config_path)?;\n294\t    writeln!(file, \"# bead-forge configuration\")?;\n295\t    writeln!(\n296\t        file,\n297\t        \"# br ignores bf-specific keys (claim_ttl_minutes, rotate_*)\"\n298\t    )?;\n299\t    file.write_all(yaml.as_bytes())?;\n300\t\n301\t    Ok(true)\n302\t}\n303\t\n304\t/// Verify forward compatibility: check that br can still open this database.\n305\t///\n306\t/// br checks that issues table column count matches exactly. We verify this\n307\t/// by checking the column count against br's expected count.\n308\t///\n309\t/// NOTE: The bf schema includes `content_hash` column which br does not have.\n310\t/// This is a known incompatibility - br will rebuild the issues table if it\n311\t/// sees this extra column. For full forward compatibility, `content_hash`\n312\t/// should be removed from the issues table schema and stored in a separate table.\n313\tfn verify_forward_compat(storage: &Storage) -> Result<bool> {\n314\t    // These are the actual columns in br's issues table (from beads_rust schema)\n315\t    // Note: labels, dependencies, comments are in separate tables, NOT columns\n316\t    let expected_br_columns = vec![\n317\t        \"id\",\n318\t        \"title\",\n319\t        \"description\",\n320\t        \"design\",\n321\t        \"acceptance_criteria\",\n322\t        \"notes\",\n323\t        \"status\",\n324\t        \"priority\",\n325\t        \"issue_type\",\n326\t        \"assignee\",\n327\t        \"owner\",\n328\t        \"estimated_minutes\",\n329\t        \"created_at\",\n330\t        \"created_by\",\n331\t        \"updated_at\",\n332\t        \"closed_at\",\n333\t        \"close_reason\",\n334\t        \"closed_by_session\",\n335\t        \"due_at\",\n336\t        \"defer_until\",\n337\t        \"external_ref\",\n338\t        \"source_system\",\n339\t        \"source_repo\",\n340\t        \"deleted_at\",\n341\t        \"deleted_by\",\n342\t        \"delete_reason\",\n343\t        \"original_type\",\n344\t        \"compaction_level\",\n345\t        \"compacted_at\",\n346\t        \"compacted_at_commit\",\n347\t        \"original_size\",\n348\t        \"sender\",\n349\t        \"ephemeral\",\n350\t        \"pinned\",\n351\t        \"is_template\",\n352\t    ];\n353\t\n354\t    storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n355\t        // Get actual column names\n356\t        let mut stmt = tx.prepare(\"PRAGMA table_info(issues)\")?;\n357\t        let mut actual_columns = Vec::new();\n358\t        let mut rows = stmt.query([])?;\n359\t        while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {\n360\t            let name: String = row.get(1)?;\n361\t            actual_columns.push(name);\n362\t        }\n363\t\n364\t        // Check that all expected br columns exist\n365\t        let mut all_present = true;\n366\t        for expected in &expected_br_columns {\n367\t            if !actual_columns.iter().any(|c| c == expected) {\n368\t                all_present = false;\n369\t                break;\n370\t            }\n371\t        }\n372\t\n373\t        // Check that there are no extra columns (br's rebuild_issues_table check)\n374\t        // bf adds `content_hash` which br doesn't have - this breaks forward compat\n375\t        if actual_columns.len() != expected_br_columns.len() {\n376\t            all_present = false;\n377\t        }\n378\t\n379\t        Ok(all_present)\n380\t    })\n381\t}\n382\t\n383\t/// Migrate from br's JSONL export, reconstructing events from git log.\n384\t///\n385\t/// This function:\n386\t/// 1. Reimports issues from issues.jsonl\n387\t/// 2. Reconstructs events by parsing git log --follow -p .beads/issues.jsonl\n388\t/// 3. Creates synthetic events for state transitions\n389\t/// 4. Optionally seeds velocity stats from reconstructed events\n390\tpub fn migrate_from_jsonl(workspace: &Path, seed_velocity: bool) -> Result<MigrateResult> {\n391\t    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace)\n392\t        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {:?}\", workspace))?;\n393\t\n394\t    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n395\t    let config = load_config(&beads_dir)?;\n396\t    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n397\t    let jsonl_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.jsonl_export);\n398\t\n399\t    // Step 1: Import issues from JSONL (rebuilds issues table)\n400\t    let storage = Storage::open_with_config(&db_path, &config)?;\n401\t    let import_result = storage.sync_from_jsonl(&jsonl_path)?;\n402\t\n403\t    // Step 2: Reconstruct events from git log\n404\t    let events_reconstructed = reconstruct_events_from_git(&storage, workspace, &jsonl_path)?;\n405\t\n406\t    // Step 3: Seed velocity stats if requested\n407\t    if seed_velocity {\n408\t        seed_velocity_from_events(&storage)?;\n409\t    }\n410\t\n411\t    // Step 4: Verify migration\n412\t    let verification = verify_migration(&storage)?;\n413\t\n414\t    Ok(MigrateResult {\n415\t        verification,\n416\t        imported: import_result.imported,\n417\t        events_reconstructed,\n418\t    })\n419\t}\n420\t\n421\t/// Reconstruct events from git log history of issues.jsonl.\n422\t///\n423\t/// Parses git log to get the JSONL file state at each commit, then\n424\t/// creates synthetic events for state transitions:\n425\t/// - New bead appearing = synthetic created event\n426\t/// - Status open->in_progress = synthetic claimed event\n427\t/// - Status->closed = synthetic closed event with duration_seconds\n428\t///\n429\t/// All synthetic events get metadata.source=git-reconstructed annotation.\n430\tfn reconstruct_events_from_git(\n431\t    storage: &Storage,\n432\t    workspace: &Path,\n433\t    jsonl_path: &Path,\n434\t) -> Result<usize> {\n435\t    // Get snapshots from git log\n436\t    let snapshots = parse_git_log_snapshots(workspace, jsonl_path)?;\n437\t\n438\t    if snapshots.is_empty() {\n439\t        return Ok(0);\n440\t    }\n441\t\n442\t    let mut events_created = 0;\n443\t\n444\t    // Process snapshots chronologically (oldest to newest)\n445\t    for window in snapshots.windows(2) {\n446\t        let prev = &window[0];\n447\t        let curr = &window[1];\n448\t\n449\t        // Find differences between snapshots and create synthetic events\n450\t        for (issue_id, current_issue) in &curr.issues {\n451\t            let prev_issue = prev.issues.get(issue_id);\n452\t\n453\t            match prev_issue {\n454\t                None => {\n455\t                    // New bead appeared - create synthetic created event\n456\t                    insert_synthetic_event(\n457\t                        storage,\n458\t                        issue_id,\n459\t                        EventType::Created,\n460\t                        None,\n461\t                        None,\n462\t                        current_issue.created_at,\n463\t                    )?;\n464\t                    events_created += 1;\n465\t\n466\t                    // If already in_progress at creation, add claimed event\n467\t                    if current_issue.status == Status::InProgress {\n468\t                        insert_synthetic_event(\n469\t                            storage,\n470\t                            issue_id,\n471\t                            EventType::StatusChanged,\n472\t                            Some(Status::Open.as_str()),\n473\t                            Some(Status::InProgress.as_str()),\n474\t                            current_issue.created_at,\n475\t                        )?;\n476\t                        events_created += 1;\n477\t                    }\n478\t\n479\t                    // If already closed at creation, add closed event\n480\t                    if current_issue.status == Status::Closed {\n481\t                        insert_synthetic_event(\n482\t                            storage,\n483\t                            issue_id,\n484\t                            EventType::Closed,\n485\t                            None,\n486\t                            current_issue.close_reason.as_deref(),\n487\t                            current_issue.closed_at.unwrap_or(current_issue.created_at),\n488\t                        )?;\n489\t                        events_created += 1;\n490\t                    }\n491\t                }\n492\t                Some(previous_issue) => {\n493\t                    // Existing bead - check for status changes\n494\t                    if previous_issue.status != current_issue.status {\n495\t                        match (previous_issue.status.clone(), current_issue.status.clone()) {\n496\t                            (Status::Open, Status::InProgress) => {\n497\t                                // open -> in_progress = claimed\n498\t                                insert_synthetic_event(\n499\t                                    storage,\n500\t                                    issue_id,\n501\t                                    EventType::StatusChanged,\n502\t                                    Some(Status::Open.as_str()),\n503\t                                    Some(Status::InProgress.as_str()),\n504\t                                    curr.commit_date,\n505\t                                )?;\n506\t                                events_created += 1;\n507\t                            }\n508\t                            (_, Status::Closed) => {\n509\t                                // any -> closed = closed event\n510\t                                insert_synthetic_event(\n511\t                                    storage,\n512\t                                    issue_id,\n513\t                                    EventType::Closed,\n514\t                                    None,\n515\t                                    current_issue.close_reason.as_deref(),\n516\t                                    curr.commit_date,\n517\t                                )?;\n518\t                                events_created += 1;\n519\t                            }\n520\t                            _ => {\n521\t                                // Other status changes - generic status_changed event\n522\t                                insert_synthetic_event(\n523\t                                    storage,\n524\t                                    issue_id,\n525\t                                    EventType::StatusChanged,\n526\t                                    Some(previous_issue.status.as_str()),\n527\t                                    Some(current_issue.status.as_str()),\n528\t                                    curr.commit_date,\n529\t                                )?;\n530\t                                events_created += 1;\n531\t                            }\n532\t                        }\n533\t                    }\n534\t\n535\t                    // Check for assignee changes\n536\t                    if previous_issue.assignee != current_issue.assignee {\n537\t                        insert_synthetic_event(\n538\t                            storage,\n539\t                            issue_id,\n540\t                            EventType::AssigneeChanged,\n541\t                            previous_issue.assignee.as_deref(),\n542\t                            current_issue.assignee.as_deref(),\n543\t                            curr.commit_date,\n544\t                        )?;\n545\t                        events_created += 1;\n546\t                    }\n547\t\n548\t                    // Check for priority changes\n549\t                    if previous_issue.priority != current_issue.priority {\n550\t                        insert_synthetic_event(\n551\t                            storage,\n552\t                            issue_id,\n553\t                            EventType::PriorityChanged,\n554\t                            Some(&previous_issue.priority.0.to_string()),\n555\t                            Some(&current_issue.priority.0.to_string()),\n556\t                            curr.commit_date,\n557\t                        )?;\n558\t                        events_created += 1;\n559\t storage,\n570\t                    issue_id,\n571\t                    EventType::Deleted,\n572\t                    None,\n573\t                    None,\n574\t                    curr.commit_date,\n575\t                )?;\n576\t                events_created += 1;\n577\t            }\n578\t        }\n579\t    }\n580\t\n581\t    Ok(events_created)\n582\t}\n583\t\n584\t/// Insert a synthetic event with metadata.source=git-reconstructed.\n585\t///\n586\t/// Silently skips insertion if the issue doesn't exist (handles deleted beads\n587\t/// from git history gracefully).\n588\tfn insert_synthetic_event(\n589\t    storage: &Storage,\n590\t    issue_id: &str,\n591\t    event_type: EventType,\n592\t    old_value: Option<&str>,\n593\t    new_value:    Ok(())\n629\t    })\n630\t}\n631\t\n632\t/// Parse git log to get snapshots of issues.jsonl at each commit.\n633\t///\n634\t/// Runs: git log --follow --date=iso-strict -- .beads/issues.jsonl\n635\t/// Then for each commit, runs: git show <commit>:path to get full contents\n636\tfn parse_git_log_snapshots(workspace: &Path, jsonl_path: &Path) -> Result<Vec<JsonlSnapshot>> {\n637\t    let relative_path = jsonl_path.strip_prefix(workspace).unwrap_or(jsonl_path);\n638\t    let path_str = relative_path.to_str().unwrap_or(\".beads/issues.jsonl\");\n639\t\n640\t    // First, get list of commits that touched this file\n641\t    let output = Command::new(\"git\")\n642\t        .args([\"log\", \"--follow\", \"--format=%H|%ci\", \"--\", path_str])\n643\t        .current_dir(workspace)\n644\t        .output()?;\n645\t\n646\t    if !output.status.success() {\n647\t        // Not in git repo or file has no history - return empty\n648\t        return Ok(Vec::new());\n649\t    }\n650\t\n651\t    let stdout = String::from_utf8(output.stdout)?;\n652\t    let mut snapshots = Vec::new();\n653\t\n654\t    // Parse each commit and get the file contents at that commit\n655\t    for line in stdout.lines() {\n656\t        let parts: Vec<&str> = line.splitn(2, '|').collect();\n657\t        if parts.len() != 2 {\n658\t            continue;\n659\t        }\n660\t\n661\t        let commit_hash = parts[0].to_string();\n662\t        let commit_date_str = parts[1];\n663\t\n664\t        // Parse the date (git log --format=%ci gives \"2024-01-01 12:00:00 +0000\")\n665\t        let commit_date = parse_git_date(commit_date_str)?;\n666\t\n667\t        // Get file contents at this commit\n668\t        let file_output = Command::new(\"git\")\n669\t            .args([\"show\", &format!(\"{}:{}\", commit_hash, path_str)])\n670\t            .current_dir(workspace)\n671\t            .output();\n672\t\n673\t        let issues = match file_output {\n674\t            Ok(out) if out.status.success() => {\n675\t                let content = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);\n676\t                parse_jsonl_issues(&content)?\n677\t            }\n678\t            _ => {\n679\t                // File didn't exist at this commit (or was empty)\n680\t                HashMap::new()\n681\t            }\n682\t        };\n683\t\n684\t        snapshots.push(JsonlSnapshot {\n685\t            commit_hash,\n686\t Ok(snapshots)\n694\t}\n695\t\n696\t/// Parse git date format \"2024-01-01 12:00:00 +0000\" to DateTime<Utc>\n697\tfn parse_git_date(date_str: &str) -> Result<DateTime<Utc>> {\n698\t    use chrono::NaiveDateTime;\n699\t    // git log --format=%ci gives \"2024-01-01 12:00:00 +0000\"\n700\t    let trimmed = date_str.trim();\n701\t    // Parse \"YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS +ZZZZ\" format\n702\t // e.g., \"worker-claude-sonnet-4-6-01\" → \"claude-sonnet-4-6\"\n740\t    if actor_lower.starts_with(\"worker-\") {\n741\t        let parts: Vec<&str> = actor_lower.split('-').collect();\n742\t parts.len() - 1 && part.parse::<u32>().is_ok() {\n753\t                    break; // Skip the trailing numeric suffix\n754\t                }\n755\t                model_parts.push(*part);\n756\t (\"unknown\".to_string(), \"unknown\".to_string())\n772\t}\n773\t\n774\t/// Seed velocity stats from reconstructed events.\n775\t///\n776\t/// Scans events table for closed events and populates let avg_seconds = Some(durations.iter().sum::<i64>() as f64 / count as f64);\n831\t\n832\t            tx.execute(\n833\t                \"INSERT OR REPLACE INTO velocity_stats (model, harness,   })\n850\t}\n851\t\n852\t/// Verify migration results.\n853\tfn verify_migration(storage: &Storage) -> Result<VerificationResult> {\n854\t    let mut errors = Vec::new();\n855\t\n856\t    // Check Workspace migration from br to bf.\n//!\n//! Release fleet: remove migration_lock\npub fn migrate(opts: MigrateOptions) -> Result<MigrateResult> {\n    let workspace = &opts.workspace_path;\n    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace)\n        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {:?}\", workspace))?;\n\n    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let Utc::now().format(\"%Y%m%d%H%M%S\")\n            )\n        );\n        println!(\"  Would apply schema migrations\");\n        println!(\"  Would prime critical_path_cache\");\n        println!(\"  Would seed config.yaml with bf defaults\");\n        println!(\"  Would verify forward/backward compatibility\");\n        return Ok(MigrateResult {\n            verification: VerificationResult { errors: vec![] },\n            imported: 0,\n            events_reconstructed: 0,\n        });\n    }\n\n    // Step 1: Pause fleet - acquire migration lock\n    println!(\"  Acquiring migration lock...\");\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n    let lock_id = acquire_migration_lock(&storage, \"bf-migrate\")?;\n    println!(\"  Migration lock acquired\");\n\n    // Ensure lock is released even if migration fails\n    let result = std::panic::catch_unwind(std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe(|| {\n        let result = inner_migrate(\n            &storage,\n            &beads_dir,\n            &db_path,\n            &config_path,\n            &opts.skip_verify,\n        );\n        // Release lock on completion (regardless of success/failure)\n        let _ = release_migration_lock(&storage, lock_id);\n        result\n    }));\n\n    match result {\n        Ok(inner_result) => inner_result,\n        Err(_) => {\n            release_migration_lock(&storage, lock_id)?;\n            Err(anyhow!(\"Migration panicked\"))\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Inner migration implementation (lock already held).\nfn inner_migrate(\n    storage: &Storage,\n    beads_dir: &std::path::Path,\n    db_path: &std::path::Path,\n    config_path: &std::path::Path,\n    skip_verify: &bool,\n) -> Result<MigrateResult> {\n    // Step 2: Backup database\n    let timestamp = Utc::now().format(\"%Y%m%d%H%M%S\");\n    let backup_path = db_path.with_extension(&format!(\"db.br-backup-{}\", timestamp));\n    println!(\n        \"  Backing up {} -> {}\",\n        db_path.display(),\n        backup_path.display()\n    );\n    std::fs::copy(db_path, &backup_path)?;\n\n    // Step 3: Apply migrations (CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS is idempotent)\n    println!(\"  Applying schema migrations...\");\n    storage.apply_migrations()?;\n\n    // Step 4: Prime critical_path_cache\n    println!(\"  Priming critical_path_cache...\");\n    let cache_result = prime_critical_path_cache(storage)?;\n\n    // Step 5: Seed config with bf defaults\n    println!(\"  Seeding config.yaml with bf defaults...\");\n    let config_updated = seed_config(config_path)?;\n\n    // Step 6 & 7: Verify forward/backward compatibility\n    let mut verification = VerificationResult { errors: vec![] };\n\n    if !*skip_verify {\n        println!(\"  Verifying migration...\");\n\n        // Forward compat: check that issues table column count matches br's expectation\n        let forward_compat_ok = verify_forward_compat(storage)?;\n        if !forward_compat_ok {\n            verification.errors.push(\n                \"Forward compatibility check failed: issues table column count mismatch\"\n                    .to_string(),\n            );\n        }\n\n        // Backward compat: run doctor check\n        let workspace = beads_dir.parent().unwrap_or(beads_dir);\n        match crate::doctor::check(workspace) {\n            Ok(doctor_result) => {\n                if !doctor_result.db_ok {\n                    verification.errors.push(\n                        \"Backward compatibility check failed: database integrity check failed\"\n                            .to_string(),\n                    );\n                }\n                if !doctor_result.issues.is_empty() {\n                    for issue in &doctor_result.issues {\n                        verification.errors.push(format!(\"Doctor check: {}\", issue));\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            Err(e) => {\n                verification\n                    .errors\n                    .push(format!(\"Backward compatibility check failed: {}\", e));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    println!(\"  Migration complete\");\n    if config_updated {\n        println!(\"  Note: config.yaml was updated with bf defaults\");\n    }\n\n    Ok(MigrateResult {\n        verification,\n        imported: 0,\n        events_reconstructed: cache_result,\n    })\n}\n\n/// Acquire migration lock to prevent concurrent claims during migration.\nfn acquire_migration_lock(storage: &Storage, locked_by: &str) -> Result<i64> {\n    let now = Utc::now();\n    let expires_at = now + chrono::Duration::hours(1); // Lock expires after 1 hour\n\n    storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n        tx.execute(\n            \"INSERT OR REPLACE INTO migration_lock (id, locked_by, locked_at, expires_at)\n             VALUES (1, ?1, ?2, ?3)\",\n            rusqlite::params![locked_by, now.to_rfc3339(), expires_at.to_rfc3339()],\n        )?;\n        Ok::<_, anyhow::Error>(1)\n    })\n}\n\n/// Release migration lock after migration completes.\nfn release_migration_lock(storage: &Storage, _lock_id: i64) -> Result<()> {\n    storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n        tx.execute(\"DELETE FROM migration_lock WHERE id = 1\", [])?;\n        Ok::<_, anyhow::Error>(())\n    })\n}\n\n/// Prime critical_path_cache for all beads.\nfn prime_critical_path_cache(storage: &Storage) -> Result<usize> {\n    storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n        // Compute all critical paths - this populates the cache\n        crate::critical_path::compute_all_critical_paths(tx)?;\n\n        // Count how many beads were cached\n        let count: i64 = tx.query_row(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM critical_path_cache\", [], |row| {\n            row.get(0)\n        })?;\n        Ok(count as usize)\n    })\n}\n\n/// Seed config.yaml with bf-specific defaults if missing.\nfn seed_config(config_path: &std::path::Path) -> Result<bool> {\n    use std::io::Write;\n\n    // Read existing config\n    let existing_content = if config_path.exists() {\n        std::fs::read_to_string(config_path)?\n    } else {\n        String::new()\n    };\n\n    // Check if bf defaults are already present\n    let has_claim_ttl = existing_content.contains(\"claim_ttl_minutes:\");\n    let has_rotate_age = existing_content.contains(\"rotate_age_days:\");\n    let has_rotate_max_size = existing_content.contains(\"rotate_max_size_mb:\");\n\n    if has_claim_ttl && has_rotate_age && has_rotate_max_size {\n        return Ok(false); // No update needed\n    }\n\n    // Parse existing config or create new one\n    let mut config: crate::config::Config = if existing_content.trim().is_empty() {\n        crate::config::Config::default()\n    } else {\n        serde_yaml::from_str(&existing_content)?\n    };\n\n    // Ensure bf defaults are set\n    if config.claim_ttl_minutes == 0 {\n        config.claim_ttl_minutes = 30;\n    }\n    if config.rotate.rotate_age_days == 0 {\n        config.rotate.rotate_age_days = 30;\n    }\n    if config.rotate.rotate_max_size_mb == 0 {\n        config.rotate.rotate_max_size_mb = 100;\n    }\n\n    // Write back\n    let yaml = serde_yaml::to_string(&config)?;\n    let mut file = std::fs::File::create(config_path)?;\n    writeln!(file, \"# bead-forge configuration\")?;\n    writeln!(\n        file,\n        \"# br ignores bf-specific keys (claim_ttl_minutes, rotate_*)\"\n    )?;\n    file.write_all(yaml.as_bytes())?;\n\n    Ok(true)\n}\n\n/// Verify forward compatibility: check that br can still open this database.\n///\n/// br checks that issues table column count matches exactly. We verify this\n/// by checking the column count against br's expected count.\n///\n/// NOTE: The bf schema includes `content_hash` column which br does not have.\n/// This is a known incompatibility - br will rebuild the issues table if it\n/// sees this extra column. For full forward compatibility, `content_hash`\n/// should be removed from the issues table schema and stored in a separate table.\nfn verify_forward_compat(storage: &Storage) -> Result<bool> {\n    // These are the actual columns in br's issues table (from beads_rust schema)\n    // Note: labels, dependencies, comments are in separate tables, NOT columns\n    let expected_br_columns = vec![\n        \"id\",\n        \"title\",\n        \"description\",\n        \"design\",\n        \"acceptance_criteria\",\n        \"notes\",\n        \"status\",\n        \"priority\",\n        \"issue_type\",\n        \"assignee\",\n        \"owner\",\n        \"estimated_minutes\",\n        \"created_at\",\n        \"created_by\",\n        \"updated_at\",\n        \"closed_at\",\n        \"close_reason\",\n        \"closed_by_session\",\n        \"due_at\",\n        \"defer_until\",\n        \"external_ref\",\n        \"source_system\",\n        \"source_repo\",\n        \"deleted_at\",\n        \"deleted_by\",\n        \"delete_reason\",\n        \"original_type\",\n        \"compaction_level\",\n        \"compacted_at\",\n        \"compacted_at_commit\",\n        \"original_size\",\n        \"sender\",\n        \"ephemeral\",\n        \"pinned\",\n        \"is_template\",\n    ];\n\n    storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n        // Get actual column names\n        let mut stmt = tx.prepare(\"PRAGMA table_info(issues)\")?;\n        let mut actual_columns = Vec::new();\n        let mut rows = stmt.query([])?;\n        while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {\n            let name: String = row.get(1)?;\n            actual_columns.push(name);\n        }\n\n        // Check that all expected br columns exist\n        let mut all_present = true;\n        for expected in &expected_br_columns {\n            if !actual_columns.iter().any(|c| c == expected) {\n                all_present = false;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Check that there are no extra columns (br's rebuild_issues_table check)\n        // bf adds `content_hash` which br doesn't have - this breaks forward compat\n        if actual_columns.len() != expected_br_columns.len() {\n            all_present = false;\n        }\n\n        Ok(all_present)\n    })\n}\n\n/// Migrate from br's JSONL export, reconstructing events from git log.\n///\n/// This function:\n/// 1. Reimports issues from issues.jsonl\n/// 2. Reconstructs events by parsing git log --follow -p .beads/issues.jsonl\n/// 3. Creates synthetic events for state transitions\n/// 4. Optionally seeds velocity stats from reconstructed events\npub fn migrate_from_jsonl(workspace: &Path, seed_velocity: bool) -> Result<MigrateResult> {\n    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace)\n        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {:?}\", workspace))?;\n\n    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n    let config = load_config(&beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let jsonl_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.jsonl_export);\n\n    // Step 1: Import issues from JSONL (rebuilds issues table)\n    let storage = Storage::open_with_config(&db_path, &config)?;\n    let import_result = storage.sync_from_jsonl(&jsonl_path)?;\n\n    // Step 2: Reconstruct events from git log\n    let events_reconstructed = reconstruct_events_from_git(&storage, workspace, &jsonl_path)?;\n\n    // Step 3: Seed velocity stats if requested\n    if seed_velocity {\n        seed_velocity_from_events(&storage)?;\n    }\n\n    // Step 4: Verify migration\n    let verification = verify_migration(&storage)?;\n\n    Ok(MigrateResult {\n        verification,\n        imported: import_result.imported,\n        events_reconstructed,\n    })\n}\n\n/// Reconstruct events from git log history of issues.jsonl.\n///\n/// Parses git log to get the JSONL file state at each commit, then\n/// creates synthetic events for state transitions:\n/// - New bead appearing = synthetic created event\n/// - Status open->in_progress = synthetic claimed event\n/// - Status->closed = synthetic closed event with duration_seconds\n///\n/// All synthetic events get metadata.source=git-reconstructed annotation.\nfn reconstruct_events_from_git(\n    storage: &Storage,\n    workspace: &Path,\n    jsonl_path: &Path,\n) -> Result<usize> {\n    // Get snapshots from git log\n    let snapshots = parse_git_log_snapshots(workspace, jsonl_path)?;\n\n    if snapshots.is_empty() {\n        return Ok(0);\n    }\n\n    let mut events_created = 0;\n\n    // Process snapshots chronologically (oldest to newest)\n    for window in snapshots.windows(2) {\n        let prev = &window[0];\n        let curr = &window[1];\n\n        // Find differences between snapshots and create synthetic events\n        for (issue_id, current_issue) in &curr.issues {\n            let prev_issue = prev.issues.get(issue_id);\n\n            match prev_issue {\n                None => {\n                    // New bead appeared - create synthetic created event\n                    insert_synthetic_event(\n                        storage,\n                        issue_id,\n                        EventType::Created,\n                        None,\n                        None,\n                        current_issue.created_at,\n                    )?;\n                    events_created += 1;\n\n                    // If already in_progress at creation, add claimed event\n                    if current_issue.status == Status::InProgress {\n                        insert_synthetic_event(\n                            storage,\n                            issue_id,\n                            EventType::StatusChanged,\n                            Some(Status::Open.as_str()),\n                            Some(Status::InProgress.as_str()),\n                            current_issue.created_at,\n                        )?;\n                        events_created += 1;\n                    }\n\n                    // If already closed at creation, add closed event\n                    if current_issue.status == Status::Closed {\n                        insert_synthetic_event(\n                            storage,\n                            issue_id,\n                            EventType::Closed,\n                            None,\n                            current_issue.close_reason.as_deref(),\n                            current_issue.closed_at.unwrap_or(current_issue.created_at),\n                        )?;\n                        events_created += 1;\n                    }\n                }\n                Some(previous_issue) => {\n                    // Existing bead - check for status changes\n                    if previous_issue.status != current_issue.status {\n                        match (previous_issue.status.clone(), current_issue.status.clone()) {\n                            (Status::Open, Status::InProgress) => {\n                                // open -> in_progress = claimed\n                                insert_synthetic_event(\n                                    storage,\n                                    issue_id,\n                                    EventType::StatusChanged,\n                                    Some(Status::Open.as_str()),\n                                    Some(Status::InProgress.as_str()),\n                                    curr.commit_date,\n                                )?;\n                                events_created += 1;\n                            }\n                            (_, Status::Closed) => {\n                                // any -> closed = closed event\n                                insert_synthetic_event(\n                                    storage,\n                                    issue_id,\n                                    EventType::Closed,\n                                    None,\n                                    current_issue.close_reason.as_deref(),\n                                    curr.commit_date,\n                                )?;\n                                events_created += 1;\n                            }\n                            _ => {\n                                // Other status changes - generic status_changed event\n                                insert_synthetic_event(\n                                    storage,\n                                    issue_id,\n                                    EventType::StatusChanged,\n                                    Some(previous_issue.status.as_str()),\n                                    Some(current_issue.status.as_str()),\n                                    curr.commit_date,\n                                )?;\n                                events_created += 1;\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n\n                    // Check for assignee changes\n                    if previous_issue.assignee != current_issue.assignee {\n                        insert_synthetic_event(\n                            storage,\n                            issue_id,\n                            EventType::AssigneeChanged,\n                            previous_issue.assignee.as_deref(),\n                            current_issue.assignee.as_deref(),\n                            curr.commit_date,\n                        )?;\n                        events_created += 1;\n                    }\n\n                    // Check for priority changes\n                    if previous_issue.priority != current_issue.priority {\n                        insert_synthetic_event(\n                            storage,\n                            issue_id,\n                            EventType::PriorityChanged,\n                            Some(&previous_issue.priority.0.to_string()),\n                            Some(&current_issue.priority.0.to_string()),\n                            curr.commit_date,\n                        )?;\n                        events_created += 1;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Check for deleted beads (tombstones)\n        for issue_id in prev.issues.keys() {\n            if !curr.issues.contains_key(issue_id) {\n                // Bead was deleted - create synthetic deleted event\n                insert_synthetic_event(\n                    storage,\n                    issue_id,\n                    EventType::Deleted,\n                    None,\n                    None,\n                    curr.commit_date,\n                )?;\n                events_created += 1;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(events_created)\n}\n\n/// Insert a synthetic event with metadata.source=git-reconstructed.\n///\n/// Silently skips insertion if the issue doesn't exist (handles deleted beads\n/// from git history gracefully).\nfn insert_synthetic_event(\n    storage: &Storage,\n    issue_id: &str,\n    event_type: EventType,\n    old_value: Option<&str>,\n    new_value: Option<&str>,\n    created_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n        // Check if issue exists before inserting event\n        let exists: bool = tx.query_row(\n            \"SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM issues WHERE id = ?1)\",\n            rusqlite::params![issue_id],\n            |row| row.get(0),\n        )?;\n\n        if !exists {\n            // Issue doesn't exist - likely deleted, skip event insertion\n            return Ok(());\n        }\n\n        tx.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO events (issue_id, event_type, actor, old_value, new_value, created_at)\n             VALUES (?1, ?2, 'git-reconstructed', ?3, ?4, ?5)\",\n            rusqlite::params![\n                issue_id,\n                event_type.as_str(),\n                old_value,\n                new_value,\n                created_at.to_rfc3339()\n            ],\n        )?;\n\n        // Add annotation marking this as git-reconstructed\n        tx.execute(\n            \"INSERT OR IGNORE INTO bead_annotations (bead_id, key, value)\n             VALUES (?1, 'metadata.source', 'git-reconstructed')\",\n            rusqlite::params![issue_id],\n        )?;\n\n        Ok(())\n    })\n}\n\n/// Parse git log to get snapshots of issues.jsonl at each commit.\n///\n/// Runs: git log --follow --date=iso-strict -- .beads/issues.jsonl\n/// Then for each commit, runs: git show <commit>:path to get full contents\nfn parse_git_log_snapshots(workspace: &Path, jsonl_path: &Path) -> Result<Vec<JsonlSnapshot>> {\n    let relative_path = jsonl_path.strip_prefix(workspace).unwrap_or(jsonl_path);\n    let path_str = relative_path.to_str().unwrap_or(\".beads/issues.jsonl\");\n\n    // First, get list of commits that touched this file\n    let output = Command::new(\"git\")\n        .args([\"log\", \"--follow\", \"--format=%H|%ci\", \"--\", path_str])\n        .current_dir(workspace)\n        .output()?;\n\n    if !output.status.success() {\n        // Not in git repo or file has no history - return empty\n        return Ok(Vec::new());\n    }\n\n    let stdout = String::from_utf8(output.stdout)?;\n    let mut snapshots = Vec::new();\n\n    // Parse each commit and get the file contents at that commit\n    for line in stdout.lines() {\n        let parts: Vec<&str> = line.splitn(2, '|').collect();\n        if parts.len() != 2 {\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        let commit_hash = parts[0].to_string();\n        let commit_date_str = parts[1];\n\n        // Parse the date (git log --format=%ci gives \"2024-01-01 12:00:00 +0000\")\n        let commit_date = parse_git_date(commit_date_str)?;\n\n        // Get file contents at this commit\n        let file_output = Command::new(\"git\")\n            .args([\"show\", &format!(\"{}:{}\", commit_hash, path_str)])\n            .current_dir(workspace)\n            .output();\n\n        let issues = match file_output {\n            Ok(out) if out.status.success() => {\n                let content = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);\n                parse_jsonl_issues(&content)?\n            }\n            _ => {\n                // File didn't exist at this commit (or was empty)\n                HashMap::new()\n            }\n        };\n\n        snapshots.push(JsonlSnapshot {\n            commit_hash,\n            commit_date,\n            issues,\n        });\n    }\n\n    // git log returns newest first, we want oldest first\n    snapshots.reverse();\n    Ok(snapshots)\n}\n\n/// Parse git date format \"2024-01-01 12:00:00 +0000\" to DateTime<Utc>\nfn parse_git_date(date_str: &str) -> Result<DateTime<Utc>> {\n    use chrono::NaiveDateTime;\n    // git log --format=%ci gives \"2024-01-01 12:00:00 +0000\"\n    let trimmed = date_str.trim();\n    // Parse \"YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS +ZZZZ\" format\n    if let Ok(dt) = NaiveDateTime::parse_from_str(trimmed, \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S %z\") {\n        return Ok(DateTime::from_naive_utc_and_offset(dt, Utc));\n    }\n\n    // Fallback: try parsing as ISO-8601\n    DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(trimmed)\n        .map(|dt| dt.with_timezone(&Utc))\n        .map_err(|_| anyhow::anyhow!(\"Failed to parse git date: {}\", date_str))\n}\n\n/// Parse JSONL content into a map of issues.\nfn parse_jsonl_issues(content: &str) -> Result<HashMap<String, Issue>> {\n    let mut issues = HashMap::new();\n    for line in content.lines() {\n        let line = line.trim();\n        if line.is_empty() {\n            continue;\n        }\n        if let Ok(issue) = serde_json::from_str::<Issue>(line) {\n            issues.insert(issue.id.clone(), issue);\n        }\n    }\n    Ok(issues)\n}\n\n/// Parse a worker ID to extract model and harness information.\n///\n/// Worker IDs follow patterns like:\n/// - \"worker-claude-sonnet-4-6-01\" → model=\"claude-sonnet-4-6\", harness=\"unknown\"\n/// - \"worker-claude-opus-4-7-02\" → model=\"claude-opus-4-7\", harness=\"unknown\"\n/// - \"claude-code-glm-4.7\" → model=\"claude-code-glm-4.7\", harness=\"unknown\"\n///\n/// Returns (model, harness) tuple.\nfn parse_worker_actor(actor: &str) -> (String, String) {\n    let actor_lower = actor.to_lowercase();\n\n    // Pattern: worker-{model}-{number}\n    // e.g., \"worker-claude-sonnet-4-6-01\" → \"claude-sonnet-4-6\"\n    if actor_lower.starts_with(\"worker-\") {\n        let parts: Vec<&str> = actor_lower.split('-').collect();\n        if parts.len() >= 3 {\n            // Rejoin parts between \"worker\" and the final numeric suffix\n            // e.g., [\"worker\", \"claude\", \"sonnet\", \"4\", \"6\", \"01\"]\n            //      → model = \"claude-sonnet-4-6\", skip \"01\"\n            let mut model_parts = Vec::new();\n            for (i, part) in parts.iter().enumerate() {\n                if i == 0 {\n                    continue; // skip \"worker\"\n                }\n                // Check if this is a numeric suffix (like \"01\", \"02\")\n                if i == parts.len() - 1 && part.parse::<u32>().is_ok() {\n                    break; // Skip the trailing numeric suffix\n                }\n                model_parts.push(*part);\n            }\n            if !model_parts.is_empty() {\n                return (model_parts.join(\"-\"), \"unknown\".to_string());\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Pattern: {model}-{number} (e.g., \"claude-code-glm-4.7\")\n    if actor_lower.contains(\"claude\") {\n        // Extract the model name - everything up to a version-like pattern\n        // or use the whole actor if no clear pattern\n        return (actor_lower, \"unknown\".to_string());\n    }\n\n    // Fallback: couldn't parse\n    (\"unknown\".to_string(), \"unknown\".to_string())\n}\n\n/// Seed velocity stats from reconstructed events.\n///\n/// Scans events table for closed events and populates velocity_stats table\n/// with duration data. Uses the actor field from events to infer model/harness.\npub fn seed_velocity_from_events(storage: &Storage) -> Result<()> {\n    storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n        // Get all closed events with duration info, prioritizing event actor\n        let mut stmt = tx.prepare(\n            \"SELECT e.issue_id, i.issue_type, e.actor, i.closed_at, i.created_at\n             FROM events e\n             INNER JOIN issues i ON i.id = e.issue_id\n             WHERE e.event_type = 'closed'\n             AND i.closed_at IS NOT NULL\n             AND e.actor IS NOT NULL\n             AND e.actor != ''\n             ORDER BY e.created_at ASC\",\n        )?;\n\n        let mut rows = stmt.query([])?;\n        let mut stats: HashMap<(String, String, String), Vec<i64>> = HashMap::new();\n\n        while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {\n            let _issue_id: String = row.get(0)?;\n            let issue_type: String = row.get(1)?;\n            let actor: String = row.get(2)?;\n            let closed_at: String = row.get(3)?;\n            let created_at: String = row.get(4)?;\n\n            let closed_dt = DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(&closed_at)?.with_timezone(&Utc);\n            let created_dt = DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(&created_at)?.with_timezone(&Utc);\n            let duration_secs = closed_dt.signed_duration_since(created_dt).num_seconds();\n\n            // Skip negative durations (data issues)\n            if duration_secs < 0 {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            // Parse actor to infer model and harness\n            let (model, harness) = parse_worker_actor(&actor);\n\n            stats.entry((model, harness, issue_type))\n                .or_default()\n                .push(duration_secs);\n        }\n\n        // Compute and insert stats\n        for ((model, harness, issue_type), mut durations) in stats {\n            if durations.is_empty() {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            durations.sort_unstable();\n            let count = durations.len();\n            let p50_seconds = Some(durations[count / 2] as i32);\n            let p90_idx = (count * 9 / 10).min(count - 1);\n            let p90_seconds = Some(durations[p90_idx] as i32);\n            let avg_seconds = Some(durations.iter().sum::<i64>() as f64 / count as f64);\n\n            tx.execute(\n                \"INSERT OR REPLACE INTO velocity_stats (model, harness, issue_type, sample_count, p50_seconds, p90_seconds, avg_seconds, last_updated)\n                 VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6, ?7, ?8)\",\n                rusqlite::params![\n                    model,\n                    harness,\n                    issue_type,\n                    count as i32,\n                    p50_seconds,\n                    p90_seconds,\n                    avg_seconds,\n Provides health checking and recovery operations for bead databases,\n//! including corruption detection and JSONL-based repair.\n\nuse crate::config::{find_beads_dir, load_metadata};\nuse crate::jsonl::{import_jsonl, stream_issues, UpsertResult};\nuse crate::storage::Storage;\nuse anyhow::{anyhow, Result};\nuse rusqlite::Connection;\nuse std::path::Path;\n\n/// Doctor check results.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct DoctorResult {\n    pub db_ok: bool,\n    pub jsonl_ok: bool,\n    pub jsonl_line_count: usize,\n    pub db_issue_count: usize,\n    pub issues: Vec<String>,\n}\n\n/// Perform a health check on the bead database and JSONL file.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `workspace_dir` - Path to the workspace root (contains .beads/)\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(DoctorResult)` - Health check results\npub fn check(workspace_dir: &Path) -> Result<DoctorResult> {\n    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace_dir)\n        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", workspace_dir.display()))?;\n    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let jsonl_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.jsonl_export);\n\n    let mut issues = Vec::new();\n    let mut db_ok = true;\n    let mut jsonl_ok = true;\n\n    // Check database\n    let (db_issue_count, db_integrity_ok) = check_database(&db_path)?;\n    if !db_integrity_ok {\n        db_ok = false;\n        issues.push(\"Database integrity check failed\".to_string());\n    }\n\n    // Check JSONL file\n    let (jsonl_line_count, jsonl_valid) = check_jsonl(&jsonl_path)?;\n    if !jsonl_valid {\n        jsonl_ok = false;\n        issues.push(\"JSONL file contains invalid lines\".to_string());\n    }\n\n    // Check consistency\n    if db_ok && jsonl_ok {\n        if check_consistency(&db_path, &jsonl_path)?.is_some() {\n            issues.push(format!(\n                \"Count mismatch: database has {} issues, JSONL has {}\",\n                db_issue_count, jsonl_line_count\n            ));\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(DoctorResult {\n        db_ok,\n        jsonl_ok,\n        jsonl_line_count,\n        db_issue_count,\n        issues,\n    })\n}\n\n/// Check database integrity.\nfn check_database(db_path: &Path) -> Result<(usize, bool)> {\n    let conn = Connection::open(db_path)?;\n\n    // Apply schema if database is new (no tables yet)\n    let needs_schema: bool = conn\n        .query_row(\n            \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='issues'\",\n            [],\n            |row| row.get::<_, i64>(0),\n        )\n        .map(|n| n == 0)\n        .unwrap_or(true);\n    if needs_schema {\n        crate::storage::schema::apply_schema(&conn)?;\n    }\n\n    // Run PRAGMA integrity_check\n    let integrity_result: String =\n        conn.query_row(\"PRAGMA integrity_check\", [], |row| row.get(0))?;\n\n    let integrity_ok = integrity_result == \"ok\";\n\n    // Count issues\n    let count: i64 = conn.query_row(\n        \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM issues WHERE deleted_at IS NULL\",\n        [],\n        |row| row.get(0),\n    )?;\n\n    Ok((count as usize, integrity_ok))\n}\n\n/// Check JSONL file validity.\nfn check_jsonl(jsonl_path: &Path) -> Result<(usize, bool)> {\n    if !jsonl_path.exists() {\n        return Ok((0, true)); // Empty workspace is valid\n    }\n\n    let mut count = 0;\n    let mut valid = true;\n\n    match stream_issues(jsonl_path) {\n        Ok(iter) => {\n            for result in iter {\n                match result {\n                    Ok(_) => count += 1,\n                    Err(e) => {\n                        valid = false;\n                        eprintln!(\"Invalid JSONL line: {}\", e);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        Err(e) => {\n            return Err(anyhow!(\"Failed to read JSONL file: {}\", e));\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok((count, valid))\n}\n\n/// Check consistency between database and JSONL.\n///\n/// Returns Some(count) if counts differ, None if they match.\nfn check_consistency(db_path: &Path, jsonl_path: &Path) -> Result<Option<usize>> {\n    let conn = Connection::open(db_path)?;\n    let db_count: i64 = conn.query_row(\n        \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM issues WHERE deleted_at IS NULL\",\n        [],\n        |row| row.get(0),\n    )?;\n\n    let jsonl_count = if jsonl_path.exists() {\n        let iter = stream_issues(jsonl_path)?;\n        iter.count() as i64\n    } else {\n        0\n    };\n\n    if db_count != jsonl_count {\n        Ok(Some(db_count as usize))\n    } else {\n        Ok(None)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Repair the database by rebuilding from JSONL.\n///\n/// This is the recovery operation when the database is corrupted or missing.\n/// The JSONL file is the authoritative source of truth.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `workspace_dir` - Path to the workspace root (contains .beads/)\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(usize)` - Number of beads imported\npub fn repair(workspace_dir: &Path) -> Result<usize> {\n    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace_dir)\n        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", workspace_dir.display()))?;\n    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let jsonl_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.jsonl_export);\n\n    // Check if JSONL exists\n    if !jsonl_path.exists() {\n        return Err(anyhow!(\n            \"Cannot repair: JSONL file not found at {}\",\n            jsonl_path.display()\n        ));\n    }\n\n    // Backup existing database if it exists\n    if db_path.exists() {\n        let backup_path = db_path.with_extension(&format!(\n            \"db.backup.{}\",\n            chrono::Utc::now().format(\"%Y%m%d%H%M%S\")\n        ));\n        std::fs::copy(&db_path, &backup_path)?;\n        eprintln!(\"Backed up existing database to {}\", backup_path.display());\n    }\n\n    // Delete old database\n    if db_path.exists() {\n        std::fs::remove_file(&db_path)?;\n    }\n\n    // Create new database and import JSONL\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    let result = import_jsonl(&jsonl_path, |issue| {\n        storage.create_issue(issue)?;\n        Ok(UpsertResult::New)\n    })?;\n\n    // Rebuild blocked cache\n    storage.rebuild_blocked_cache()?;\n\n    Ok(result.imported)\n}\n\n/// Rebuild the blocked issues cache.\n///\n/// This materialized view should be rebuilt after dependency or status changes.\npub fn rebuild_cache(workspace_dir: &Path) -> Result<()> {\n    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace_dir)\n        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", workspace_dir.display()))?;\n    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n    storage.rebuild_blocked_cache()?;\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Reclaim stale in_progress beads.\n///\n/// Resets beads that have been in_progress for longer than the TTL\n/// back to open status.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `workspace_dir` - Path to the workspace root (contains .beads/)\n/// * `ttl_minutes` - TTL in minutes (default from config is 30)\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(usize)` - Number of beads reclaimed\npub fn reclaim_stale(workspace_dir: &Path, ttl_minutes: i64) -> Result<usize> {\n    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace_dir)\n        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", workspace_dir.display()))?;\n    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n\n    let storage =
